6282fb448b7351512f00d0fe4a156fc4b5bc1dfd: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
\cos \theta &= \frac{\vec{a}*\vec{b}}{|\vec{a}||\vec{b}|} \\
&= \frac{(0*5)+(3*0)+(4*12)}{\sqrt{ 0^2+3^2+4^2 }\sqrt{ 5^2+0^2+12^2 }} \\
&= \frac{48}{65} \\

V &= \frac{nC+mD}{m+n} \\
&= \frac{2C+3D}{3+2} \\
&= \frac{2\vec{c}+3\vec{d}}{5} \\
&= \frac{2}{5}\vec{c}+\frac{3}{5}\vec{d}
\end{aligned}$$

310f3d37deeeac136c2e001b87770cf99ec0d277: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
\vec{UV} &= V-U \\
&= \left( \frac{2}{5}\vec{c}+\frac{3}{5}\vec{d} \right)-\frac{4}{5}\vec{c} \\
&= \color{#2f9e44}-\frac{2}{5}\vec{c}+\frac{3}{5}\vec{d}
\end{aligned}$$

9e1a6ebf794590edff60896392f1143c37b82aad: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
\vec{DE} &= \vec{E}-\vec{D} = (1+1,2-5,-4-2) = \color{#2f9e44}2i-3j-6k \\
|\vec{DE}| &= \sqrt{ 2^2+(-3)^2+(-6)^2 } = \color{#2f9e44}7 \\
\vec{EF} &= \vec{F}-\vec{E} = (3-1,-4-2,-1+4) = \color{#2f9e44}2i-6j+3k \\ \\
|\vec{DE}| &= 7 \\
|\vec{EF}| &= \sqrt{ 2^2+(-6)^2+3^2 } = 7 \\
\end{aligned}$$

9193e6499df6a4d4c625ec8ff9989954536b0567: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
|\vec{PQ}| &= \sqrt{ (5-0)^2+(12-0)^2+(0-0)^2 } = \color{#2f9e44}13 \\
|\vec{PR}| &= \sqrt{ (0-0)^2+(8-0)^2+(15-0)^2 } = \color{#2f9e44}17 \\
|\vec{QR}| &= \sqrt{ (5-0)^2+(12-8)^2+(0-15)^2 } = \color{#2f9e44}\sqrt{ 266 } \\
\frac{1}{|\vec{PQ}|}\vec{PQ} &= \frac{1}{13}(5,12,0) = \color{#2f9e44}\frac{5}{13}i+\frac{12}{13}j
\end{aligned}$$
